## 🧭 Reflow Risk (WCAG 1.4.10)

Page-level indicator: resizes to 320 CSS pixels wide and checks for horizontal overflow. This is a risk indicator, not a conformance verdict — see [Behavioral Accessibility Automation](https://github.com/mgifford/ACCESSIBILITY.md/blob/main/examples/BEHAVIORAL_ACCESSIBILITY_AUTOMATION.md) for what it can and cannot detect.

#### Pattern 1: Affects 20 page(s) - 40 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [button-name](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/button-name?application=playwright)
**Impact**: critical
**Issue**: Buttons must have discernible text

**HTML Pattern**:

```html
			" aria-expanded="false">
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
.dark\:hover\:text-gray-600
```

**How to Replicate**:

## Detailed Failure Information (axe)

### https://huggingface.co

#### Rule: [button-name](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/button-name?application=playwright)
**Impact**: critical
**WCAG**: [SC 4.1.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/name-role-value) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-17
- Message: Buttons must have discernible text
- HTML: `<button type="button" class="px-2 py-0.5 hover:text-gray-500 dark:hover:text-gray-600 flex items-center 			" aria-expanded="false">`
- XPath: `.dark\:hover\:text-gray-600`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-11-17
- Message: Buttons must have discernible text
- HTML: `<button type="button" class="px-2 py-0.5 hover:text-gray-500 dark:hover:text-gray-600 flex items-center 			" aria-expanded="false">`
- XPath: `.dark\:hover\:text-gray-600`

#### Rule: [color-contrast](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/color-contrast?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.3](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-minimum) (Level AA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-11-17
- Message: Elements must meet minimum color contrast ratio thresholds
- HTML: `<span class="truncate">Search models, datasets, users...</span>`
- XPath: `.form-input-alt > .truncate`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-11-17
- Message: Elements must meet minimum color contrast ratio thresholds
- HTML: `<kbd aria-hidden="true" class="ml-auto flex-none whitespace-nowrap rounded-sm bg-gray-100 px-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-gray-400 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:text-gray-500 max-md:hidden">Ctrl K</kbd>`
- XPath: `kbd`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-11-17
- Message: Elements must meet minimum color contrast ratio thresholds
- HTML: `<p class="text-smd text-gray-400 2xl:text-base">Starting at $0.60/hour for GPU</p>`
- XPath: `.px-5.py-7.md\:px-8 > .sm\:gap-x-3\.5.gap-x-2\.5.whitespace-nowrap > .\32 xl\:text-base.text-smd`

**Failure 4:**
- First identified: 2026-11-17
- Message: Elements must meet minimum color contrast ratio thresholds
- HTML: `<kbd aria-hidden="true" class="ml-auto flex-none whitespace-nowrap rounded-sm bg-gray-100 px-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-gray-400 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:text-gray-500 max-md:hidden">Ctrl K</kbd>`
- XPath: `kbd`

#### Rule: [heading-order](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/heading-order?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate

**Failure 1:**
